- **sci**: sci-adk research orchestration hub. Routes /sci subcommands (plan, experiment, publish, package, verify, status, replicate) or a natural-language intent to research workers, loads the matching science-workflow knowledge library, and gates every conclusion through `sci-adk verify`. Agents propose; the engine judges. ([ccy5123/sci-adk](https://github.com/ccy5123/sci-adk/tree/HEAD/src/sci_adk/templates/research-workspace/.claude/skills/sci) · ★1)
- **sci**: Keeps coding agents on track in long, multi-step coding work. Use when starting, continuing or resuming multi-step work in a real codebase (features, bug fixes, refactors, migrations, performance work, scientific or numerical code; Python, Rust, JavaScript/TypeScript or any other language), after a conversation was summarized or compacted, or when work has lost direction. Not for one-line edits, questions or read-only exploration. Keeps the plan, task status, test results, ruled-out causes and codebase notes on disk; runs each task's acceptance test, earlier tasks' checks, saved reference out… ([Nag681/sci-harness](https://github.com/Nag681/sci-harness/blob/HEAD/SKILL.md))
- **sci-30s-storyboard**: 输入任意科普文案，输出 30 秒 AI 视频分镜提示词：固定 6 个镜头、外国真人主讲 + 拟人化生物配合解说，每条画面描述可直接粘贴进 AI 视频/绘图工具。当用户提供自然、生物、历史、科学类科普文案，要求生成短视频分镜、画面提示词或 AI 视频脚本时使用。 ([canyexiren/sci-30s-storyboard](https://github.com/canyexiren/sci-30s-storyboard/blob/HEAD/SKILL.md))
- **sci-abstract**: Use when writing the two front-matter artifacts Science (AAAS) demands — the one-sentence summary for the table of contents and the ≤125-word single-paragraph abstract — both readable by any scientist and both leading with the quantified advance rather than the method. - **sci-algorithm-drawio**: 先按具体算法与变体联网查找原始示意图，实际查看原图、图注与对应说明，完成参考到本图的适配核验；再为数学建模论文直接生成原生可编辑 Draw.io 流程图、算法原理图、机制图和方法框架图。结构先行，冻结有依据的连接关系，按最终纸面尺寸定字号，再以内容自适应框体与紧凑间距排版；每条箭头绑定正确对象与命名端口。黑白灰主导，允许少量辅助色，中文短标签优先，图内不放公式。禁止先画框再凑关系、乱加反馈、缩字塞图和大面积无意义留白。不调用生图、不经 PPTX 或 Mermaid 中转。 ([SimonAN01/math-modeling-infra](https://github.com/SimonAN01/math-modeling-infra/tree/HEAD/skills/sci-algorithm-drawio) · ★1)

- **sci-code-audit**: [STATUS: review] [CONFIDENCE: high] [REVIEWED: 2026-05-24] 10-layer audit of scientific/research code — answers "can we trust the code that produced these results?" Companion to a research-methodology audit and code-materiality (bug triage). Covers: silent fallbacks, invariant tests, control validity, data provenance, statistical interpretation, documentation drift, reproducibility. Triggers: /sci-code-audit, "code audit", "найди что ещё упустили", "audit code", "code hardening", "trust the code", "аудит кода", "могли упустить", "что ещё проверить", "layers of risk", "scientific code review",… ([sergeeey/Claude-cod-top-2026](https://github.com/sergeeey/Claude-cod-top-2026/tree/HEAD/skills/extensions/sci-code-audit) · ★9)
- **sci-code-exec**: Scientific code generation and execution skill (requirement-to-code coverage mapping / cleaning rules declared upfront / execution tracing with command-version-seed-exit / independent numeric recompute / anomaly registry without silent fixes). Turns a scientific data analysis requirement into runnable stdlib-only scripts, executes them with full reproducible traces (command, python version, seed, exit code, log path), independently recomputes every declared statistic from the raw data under the declared cleaning rules, maps every requirement to a code artifact, and registers missing/duplicate… ([hehekevin/boerproduct](https://github.com/hehekevin/boerproduct/tree/HEAD/week4/analysis-%E6%95%B0%E6%8D%AE%E5%88%86%E6%9E%90/challenge-9ec929fe_%E7%A7%91%E7%A0%94%E4%BB%A3%E7%A0%81%E7%94%9F%E6%88%90%E4%B8%8E%E6%89%A7%E8%A1%8C))
- **sci-communication**: Create science outreach content -- blog posts, tutorials, concept explainers, lay summaries, newsletters, social threads, and press releases from any source. Accepts papers, concepts, URLs, datasets, or personal expertise as input. Includes visual generation for inline diagrams and illustrations. Triggers on: "blog post", "tutorial", "explain this concept", "lay summary", "science blog", "write a tutorial about", "newsletter", "social thread", "press release", "explain for non-scientists", "teach this", "gentle introduction", "explainer", "educational content", "science communication", "scico… ([krmdel/organon](https://github.com/krmdel/organon/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/sci-communication) · ★8)

- **sci-diagram-pptx**: Reconstruct or repair user-provided scientific and academic schematics as visually faithful, native editable PowerPoint (.pptx) files with executable build source; also inspect existing PPTX files for meaningful fidelity and editability defects. Use for 复刻、还原、临摹、修复或检查科研框架图、技术路线图、科研流程图、机制图、算法流程图、科研系统结构图、学术概念模型、结构化科研信息图，以及包含数学符号或公式的学术图示. Do not use for quantitative data visualizations or statistical plots whose meaning is encoded by axes, scales, legends, or data-driven geometry; general business diagrams; organization charts; ordinary deck design; OCR-only extraction; or simple image placement. ([studio-yebisu/yaoyorozu-ai-toolbox-slides](https://github.com/studio-yebisu/yaoyorozu-ai-toolbox-slides/tree/HEAD/examples/repositories/sci-diagram-reconstruction/upstream) · ★12 · 6 copies)
- **sci-diagram-reconstruction-ja**: 科学模式図の画像を、元の文言・包含・方向を保つ編集可能なPowerPointへ復元する。既存科学図PPTXの修復・変更しない検査は別経路。統計グラフ、一般業務図、組織図、通常のデッキ制作、OCRのみ、画像貼付のみには使わない。 ([studio-yebisu/yaoyorozu-ai-toolbox-slides](https://github.com/studio-yebisu/yaoyorozu-ai-toolbox-slides/tree/HEAD/examples/repositories/sci-diagram-reconstruction) · ★12 · 3 copies)

- **sci-extract**: Read an academic paper end to end and extract professional research insights, figures, metadata, and critique. Use this skill whenever the user shares a scientific paper, review paper, survey paper, systematic review, meta-analysis, scoping review, arXiv link, DOI, PDF, or pasted paper text and asks to read, summarize, analyze, extract, digest, review, critique, or explain it.
